## Rules to keep
- Nothing is "verified" without repo code, tests and release evidence.
- Company roles (investor, CS, revops) never see student records.
- Status, not certifications we don't hold. Cite every external number.

Vanderbilt pilot NOT YET SIGNED (founder, 2026-10-11) — earlier note said signed; requirements if it proceeds: real student records, DPA/FERPA addendum, HECVAT (institution-required version), SAML SSO, VPAT/ACR, cyber + E&O insurance required (not held); dates not set. Hosting Vercel + Supabase; AI never sees student records. Setup budget $5k–$20k. Vanderbilt name cleared for public use.
